# docs/ — Documentation Lifecycle Registry

> **Provenance:** created Phase 236 (2026-09-19) by the owner-approved Documentation & Context
> Architecture Migration Plan §7 — the sanctioned exception to AGENTS.md §12.5's no-new-docs law
> (plan: `docs/archive/DOCS-CONTEXT-MIGRATION-PLAN-2026-09-19.md`).
> **Law:** any new documentation file lands WITH its row here in the SAME commit (§3.8 parity);
> a status that stops being true is a bug — check M re-verifies this table on every push.
> **Ph 289 (2026-09-28 — ARCH-REMEDIATION, audit P1-4):** the hand-maintained Last-updated
> column is RETIRED — git is the single source of every date (`scripts/docs_audit.py` derives
> and prints them in its report; nothing polices prose dates anymore). Check M is now
> BIDIRECTIONAL: every row's paths exist AND every top-level `docs/*.md` carries a row.
> **Agent session reading budget (§3.6, resized Phase 292):**
> `STATE.md` (~11 KB) → `AGENTS.md` §1–§4 + §12 (~17 KB — the session read; §5–§11 are
> on-demand binding law) → top-3 `worklog.md` entries
> → everything else ON DEMAND via STATE's source-of-truth map. Do not read this file top-to-bottom
> in a normal session — jump to the row you need.

## Registry — core context sphere (repo root)

| File | Role | Status | Law-ref |
|---|---|---|---|
| `STATE.md` | Living status — FIRST file of every session; state, not history (phase ladder ≤ 2 rows — Ph 289) | LIVE | §3.6/§3.8 |
| `AGENTS.md` | Binding law for every agent (§12.5.1: SHA optional post-push — one task = one commit, Ph 288) | LIVE | §1–§12.10 |
| `worklog.md` | Task log — the live file IS the active window (hard cap ≤12 entries / ≤128KB, check H5 + `scripts/worklog_rotate.py`, Ph 288), newest on top; full history in the archive | LIVE | §12.5.1 |
| `README.md` | Front door + feature law surface | LIVE | §3.8 |
| `DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md` | Onboarding + architecture | LIVE | — |
| `SECURITY.md` | Security policy | LIVE | §7 |
| `DESIGN.md` | Design system — THE binding design law (single source; the former mirrors are pointer-only since Ph 292 — a UI change touches DESIGN.md + globals.css only) | LIVE | — |
| `CONTRIBUTING.md` | Contribution policy (static) | LIVE | §12.5 exception |
| `CHANGELOG.md` | Pointer to per-phase tracking (STATE/worklog) | LIVE | §12.5 exception |
| `archive/PROGRESS.md` + `archive/QA_CHECKLIST.md` | Phase-115 merged status files, verbatim | FROZEN (docs_audit J guards zero commits since 2026-09-16) | §3.8 |
| `archive/PROGRESS_ARCHIVE.md` · `archive/WORKLOG_ARCHIVE.md` · `archive/QA_CHECKLIST_ARCHIVE.md` | Append-only history overflow (received the Ph-288 worklog rotation: 147 entries verbatim; the Ph-289 STATE history blocks) | ARCHIVE — append-only | §3.8 |

| `docs/BLOG-WEEKLY-COMPENSATION-REVIEW-2026-11-13.md` | **The BLOG-WEEKLY-COMPENSATION-REVIEW governing review (owner order 2026-11-13 — مراجعة منطق التعويض الأسبوعي وحسم التناقض الظاهري بين المدد بالكود والاختبارات لا وصف التقارير):** حسم المدد الثلاثة بفصل الطبقات — 90د = عتبة كشف لكل فتحة (الغياب قبل ساعتها+90 «متأخر» وبعده «فائت») · 23:40 UTC اليومية = قناة التعويض ونطاقها أسبوع ISO ذاته فقط (بعد التدحرج تُفقد الفتحة من الموزّع نهائيًا) · 144س = أهلية استهلاك صفوف بP0 (ADOPT/JOIN/RESUME) لا مؤقت تعويض — الموزّع لا يقرأها إطلاقًا · التسلسل الزمني لكل فتحة محسوبًا بدوال المستودع نفسها (`scripts/weekly-cadence-timeline-verify.mts`): أول إطلاقة تعويض بنفس يوم كل فتحة (بعد سماح AR بـ17س10د وبعد سماح EN بعشر دقائق) وآخر فرصة الأحد 23:40 · إثبات حرفي لادعاء فتحة الثلاثاء EN (22:00→سماح 23:30→إطلاقة 23:40 بهامش 10 دقائق؛ topUpNow=1؛ التوأم يُتبنى بـ~42.7س؛ المتأخر ≤23:40 يُحتسب in-flight وسباق >100د يُغلق بحارس اليوم) · **الحكم: آلية التعويض تعمل كما صُممت** (كشفًا وتوقيتًا وسقفًا ≤1/لغة/إطلاق وحصصًا على المنشور بأسبوع الإنشاء) — **وعيب واحد مكتشف بطبقة استهلاك الأزواج لا بالموزّع:** تبنّي `findAdoptablePairRow` «الأحدث أولًا» يُبطل ضمان 137س العابر للأسبوع (بفشل مركّب: فتحة الجمعة + كل تعويضات نهاية الأسبوع، تستهلك فتحة الثلاثاء التالية التوأم الأحدث 41س ويجوع توأم الخميس حتى 209س>144س فيُيتم بمقالة عربية بلا hreflang بصمت — الاختبار الحالي يثبت حساب النافذة لا الاختيار) · الإصلاح الأصغر محدد باختبارين مثبتين (قلب الترتيب FIFO ب`src/lib/blog-queue.ts` + توسيع اختبار السطر 335 ليثبت الاختيار) **ولم يُنفَّذ — ينتظر موافقة المالك** | **REVIEW 2026-11-13 — docs-only + مسبار قراءة-فقط (86/86 اختبارًا مستهدفًا أخضر)؛ صفر تعديل كود/جدولة/مقالات؛ تصحيح مؤرخ ب§4 من تقرير الإيقاع الحاكم** |
| `docs/BLOG-WEEKLY-COMPENSATION-FIX-2026-11-13.md` | **The BLOG-WEEKLY-COMPENSATION-FIX governing report (owner approval 2026-11-13 «موافق على تنفيذ الاصلاح» على الإصلاح الأصغر المحدد ب§6 من مراجعة التعويض الأسبوعي):** قلب ترتيب `findAdoptablePairRow` ب`src/lib/blog-queue.ts` إلى تصاعدي (**FIFO** — الأقدم الصالح أولًا، الأقرب لانتهاء الـ144س) فتصبح استحالة تجويع التوائم بنيوية — سيناريو §5 المركّب (فتحة الجمعة + كل تعويضات نهاية الأسبوع فشلت) لم يعد ييتم زوجًا: الثلاثاء W+1 يستهلك التوأم الأقدم بـ137س والجمعة التالية تجد الأحدث بـ113س **والزوجان يكملان** (كان «الأحدث أولًا» يأكل الأحدث 41س ويجيع الأقدم حتى 209س>144س فيُيتم بلا hreflang) · نطاق التعديل محصور بADOPT وحدها: `findResumableRow`/`findResumePairCandidate` بقيتا «الأحدث» عن قصد (الاستئناف يريد أحدث عمل معلق) ومثبتتان باختبار تباين · اختباران مثبتان أُكد أحمرهما قبل الإصلاح (4/6 فشل) وأخضرهما بعده: عقد الترتيب بمسبار حاكٍ لترتيب PostgREST فعليًا (`src/lib/__tests__/blog-adopt-order.test.ts`) + توسيع اختبار السيناريو ب`src/lib/__tests__/blog-weekly-cadence.test.ts` ليثبت **الاختيار** لا النافذة فقط · البوابات: tsc صفر · eslint صفر أخطاء · vitest كاملًا 5,959/5,959 (222 ملفًا، +6 اختبارات) · مسبار التسلسل الزمني حُدِّث قسم B ليعكس FIFO · التحصين الاختاري (تكرار ADOPT على المرشحين الخمسة) بقي قرارًا منفصلًا للمالك لم يُنفَّذ | **EXECUTED 2026-11-13 — سطر كود واحد + اختباران؛ صفر مساس بالحراس/الجدولة/الكرون/المقالات/البيانات** |

AR pages equally carry zero EN links — internal link equity never crosses the language boundary on a site whose external authority is near-zero) + the documented Bing-WMT verification gap (most AI-search tools ride Bing's index) + the younger AR surface age with remediated historical AR quality debt · **(2) why the UI takes seconds to settle:** four deferred-by-design client paths compound — R-1 the post-hydration language/direction flip (`UrlLocaleSync` reads localStorage mhe:lang and re-stamps `<html lang dir>` + all 92 useI18n consumers for non-/ar URLs; LIVE-MEASURED: en/ltr at t≈461ms → ar/rtl at t≈593ms, and on `/` the final state is a PERMANENT MIXED page — English URL-pinned content + Arabic chrome + RTL, with no mechanism returning the user to a full Arabic page) · R-2 deliberately deferred session restore (`AuthRouteTrigger` defers startAuth to requestIdleCallback capped 2,500ms for returning users + dynamic data-layer chunk + getSession + fetchProfile → the logged-out→logged-in header flip at ~1.5-4.5s — documented Phase-182 LCP trade-off, its perceptual cost now measured) · R-3 late-filling content islands (homepage BlogSection renders null until deferIdle(2500)+Supabase REST fetch — visible layout shift; FeaturedCoachesStrip same class) · R-5 the (app) group's static `lang="en" dir="ltr"` root (measured on /auth: SSR zero Arabic chars → full AR/RTL flip post-hydration) — while **the THEME is proven flash-free** (pre-paint script, verified in both preference directions; the perceived wrong-mode flash attributed to the RTL re-layout and the SW_UPDATED full reload) · R-6 dead state layer: the `mhe:locale` cookie is written by middleware but read by NOTHING (grep-proven) and stripped by the CF edge on public paths (measured) · **causality verdict: the two problems are NOT directly causal — they share ONE architectural ancestor** (URL-pinned static SSR that protects crawling/caching + deferred client state that pays the flash cost), and any fix that dynamic-renders by cookie would directly damage problem #1 · the prioritized fix plan F-1→F-7 (P0: crawlable footer language-cross link; P1: decide the returning-AR-user UX — respect-URL-language vs guarded 307 redirect; P1: shrink the logged-out window; P2: server-provided blog carousel props, pre-paint dir script for (app), remove the dead cookie; now the FIRST change arms one 500ms timer (EVO_SAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS), later changes only refresh the pending snapshot ref, and the fire reads the ref at fire time so it always writes the LATEST state (≤2 writes/sec mid-stream instead of one per token — coalescing, never a stale closure). Convergence per the plan's «الحفظ عند final/idle/الإغلاق» arm: pagehide + visibilitychange→hidden FLUSH the pending snapshot immediately (a hard tab close loses at most the last <500ms), and the m3 sign-out wipe stays AIRTIGHT under the debounce — the reset handler CANCELS the pending snapshot (drop, never flush: flushing there would re-persist the pre-signout conversation over the removed mirror — the exact m3 leak) with the empty state landing ≤500ms later; the 2026-08-27 hydration gate is untouched (no write before the stored history is read). Canary src/lib/__tests__/evo-chat-save-debounce.test.tsx (12 tests: source pins — the 500ms constant + the armed effect + extinct synchronous write + hydration gate + flush listeners + cancel-not-flush reset wiring; jsdom behavior against the REAL provider through a FAITHFUL SSE fetch mock — 30 deltas = ZERO writes mid-stream, exactly ONE write at the 500ms boundary carrying the post-final state, pagehide/hidden flush once, and the LIVE sign-out funnel (resetEvoChatOnSignOut) never lets the pre-signout conversation reach the mirror); the canary also pins two honest harness laws discovered while building it: fixture seeding must ride the original setItem (spy captured seeding as a provider write) and every mounted provider is force-unmounted in afterEach (a failed test orphans its provider whose live window listeners leak spurious writes into later tests) · **W1-4c EXECUTED at Phase 340** (2026-10-02, owner order «نفذ البند التالى»): the meal-planner food search (FoodSearchInput — the ONE implementation serving /meal-planner AND /ar/meal-planner; the AR route re-exports this page, exactly as the audit's «نفس الملف» note says) now rides an AbortController per query (B-03): the debounced fetch fired but the in-flight call was never cancelled nor numbered, so a slow stale «chick» response resolving after «chicken» landed its OLDER results over the correct ones — and the unguarded finally-block of the orphaned request cleared the NEWER query's spinner mid-flight (the same race's second face, closed by the same fix). Now every effect run builds its own controller and passes its signal to fetch; the effect cleanup (every query-changing keystroke, and unmount) aborts the superseded request; ownership guards keep an aborted request from writing ANYTHING — not results, not open, and not loading (a late-body belt drops a stale body that slipped past the transport abort before it could land), and the empty-query branch now drops the spinner too (no successor query exists to own it — without this the guarded finally would leave it stuck forever); the 300ms debounce, the lang=en/ar URL law, and pick() are untouched. Fix (e8cc613e): redirectOrigin = NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL (now RUNTIME-staged in deploy-aws.yml — 25 keys, was build-only) \|\| requestUrl.origin (dev-only fallback), all 5 redirect call sites; repairs Google OAuth AND password-recovery links; fs-contract guard test prevents regression; relative redirects proven impossible (500 "URL is malformed") | **EXECUTED** (commit e8cc613e; deploy run #9 38027909905; live-proven 05:39Z: Location was https://localhost:3000/ → now https://alkemos.com/) |

Other registries (outside `docs/`): `supabase/migrations/INDEX.md` — binding migration ledger (LIVE, guarded by `docs_parity.py`) · `.env.example` — environment reference (LIVE).

## How to keep this registry true

1. **New doc lands → new row in the same commit** (name it in the commit body too). Point-in-time reports (audits/plans/execution logs) are **BORN ARCHIVED** — they land directly in `docs/archive/` with their row here (Ph 291 law, audit P3-1: the live `docs/` surface is active reference only).
2. **Point-in-time docs** (audits/plans) get their status flipped to EXECUTED when their findings land, with the closing phase numbers.
3. **Dates are NOT hand-maintained** (Ph 289): git is the single source — `scripts/docs_audit.py` check M verifies this table bidirectionally on every push (paths exist · every `docs/*.md` registered) and its REPORT derives each file's last-commit date. No column, no bumping, no fix-up commits.
4. A doc that stops being cited anywhere and describes nothing live is FROZEN-to-archive material (§3.8) — move it, don't delete history.
